Under TC 661.003(c), it is an exception to the protective headgear requirement if the person is at least ______ years old and has successfully completed a motorcycle operator training and safety course, or is covered by a ______ plan providing medical benefits for collision-related injuries.

**Answer:** 21 | health insurance

**Explanation:** The age-21-plus exception requires either completion of a training course or qualifying health insurance coverage.

---

### Q616 — Motorcycles
**LO:** 22.22 · **Source:** TC 661.003(c)

Under TC 661.003(c), the protective headgear exception applies only to a person who is at least what age, in addition to meeting the training or insurance requirement?

- [x] 21 years old
- [ ] 18 years old
- [ ] 25 years old
- [ ] 30 years old

**Explanation:** 21 is the minimum age for the headgear exception.

---

### Q617 — Motorcycles
**LO:** 22.22 · **Source:** BPOC 22.22

Per BPOC 22.22 lecture content, Street Bikes range from 250cc to ______ cc engines, while Cruiser Class ("top end") motorcycles average 1100cc to ______ cc in size.

**Answer:** 1100 | 1800

**Explanation:** Street Bikes (250-1100cc) and Cruiser Class bikes (1100-1800cc) are two of the street motorcycle classes officers may encounter, per the lecture material.

---

### Q618 — Motorcycles
**LO:** 22.22 · **Source:** BPOC 22.22

Per BPOC 22.22 lecture content, the Cruiser Class of motorcycles averages what engine size range?

- [x] 1100cc to 1800cc
- [ ] 250cc to 1100cc
- [ ] 50cc to 250cc
- [ ] 1800cc to 2500cc

**Explanation:** Cruiser Class bikes average 1100-1800cc, above the Street Bike range of 250-1100cc.

---

### Q619 — Motorcycles
**LO:** 22.22 · **Source:** Penal Code Sec. 71.01(d) (BPOC 22.22)

Under Penal Code Sec. 71.01(d), a "criminal street gang" is three or more persons having a common identifying sign or symbol or an identifiable ______ who continuously or regularly associate in the commission of ______ activities.

**Answer:** leadership | criminal

**Explanation:** This is the legal definition officers use to distinguish a criminal street gang from an ordinary motorcycle club.

---

### Q620 — Motorcycles
**LO:** 22.22 · **Source:** Penal Code Sec. 71.01(d) (BPOC 22.22)

Under Penal Code Sec. 71.01(d), a "criminal street gang" requires at least how many persons?

- [x] Three or more
- [ ] Two or more
- [ ] Five or more
- [ ] Ten or more

**Explanation:** Three or more persons is the statutory threshold, along with a common sign/symbol or identifiable leadership and continuous/regular association in criminal activity.

---

### Q621 — Motorcycles
**LO:** 22.22 · **Source:** BPOC 22.22

Per the BPOC 22.22 7-Step Violator Contact Protocol, after the statement of the action to be taken and taking that action, the officer explains what the violator must do, and finally the officer must ______.

**Answer:** leave

**Explanation:** "Leave" is the final step of the 7-Step Violator Contact Protocol, following greeting, statement of violation, ID/condition check, statement of action, taking action, and explanation.

---

### Q622 — Motorcycles
**LO:** 22.22 · **Source:** BPOC 22.22

Under the BPOC 22.22 7-Step Violator Contact Protocol, which step comes LAST, after the officer explains what the violator must do?

- [ ] Take action stated
- [ ] Statement of action to be taken
- [x] Leave
- [ ] Identification of driver and check of conditions

**Explanation:** "Leave" is the seventh and final step of the protocol, following greeting, statement of violation, ID/condition check, statement of action, taking action, and explanation.

---

### Q623 — Motorcycles
**LO:** 22.22 · **Source:** BPOC 22.22

Per the BPOC 22.22 7-Step Violator Contact Protocol, the very first step is a ______ and identification of agency.

**Answer:** greeting

**Explanation:** The protocol opens with a greeting and identification of agency, before stating the violation, checking identification/conditions, stating and taking action, explaining next steps, and leaving.

---

### Q624 — Motorcycles
**LO:** 22.22 · **Source:** BPOC 22.22

Under the BPOC 22.22 7-Step Violator Contact Protocol, which step comes FIRST?

- [x] Greeting and identification of agency
- [ ] Statement of violation committed
- [ ] Identification of driver and check of conditions
- [ ] Statement of action to be taken

**Explanation:** The protocol opens with a greeting and identification of agency, before stating the violation, checking identification/conditions, stating and taking action, explaining next steps, and leaving.

---

### Q625 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.002(b)

Under TC 681.002(b), a disabled parking placard must display the international symbol of access at least ______ inches in height, shown white on a blue shield for a permanent disability or white on a ______ shield for a temporary disability.

**Answer:** three | red

**Explanation:** Blue shields mark permanent disabilities; red shields mark temporary disabilities, both with a symbol at least three inches tall.

---

### Q626 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.002(b)

Under TC 681.002(b), a disabled parking placard issued for a temporary disability displays the international symbol of access as:

- [x] White on a red shield
- [ ] White on a blue shield
- [ ] Blue on a white shield
- [ ] Red on a white shield

**Explanation:** Red shield = temporary disability; blue shield = permanent disability.

---

### Q627 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.0031(b)

Under TC 681.0031(b), the county assessor-collector shall record on a disabled parking placard the county number, the first ______ digits of the applicant's driver's license, personal identification card, or military identification number, and the applicant's ______.

**Answer:** four | initials

**Explanation:** The placard's identifying code combines the county number, the first four digits of the applicant's ID number, and the applicant's initials.

---

### Q628 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.0031(b)

Under TC 681.0031(b), the county assessor-collector records on a disabled parking placard the county number, the applicant's initials, and:

- [x] The first four digits of the applicant's driver's license, personal ID card, or military ID number
- [ ] The applicant's full driver's license number
- [ ] The applicant's date of birth
- [ ] The applicant's Social Security number

**Explanation:** Only the first four digits of the identifying number are recorded, not the full number.

---

### Q629 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.006(a)-(b)

Under TC 681.006, a vehicle displaying special disability license plates or a disabled parking placard may be parked for an ______ period in a designated disability parking space, and the owner is exempt from payment of a fee or penalty for parking at a ______.

**Answer:** unlimited | meter

**Explanation:** Both an unlimited parking-duration privilege and a parking-meter fee exemption attach to qualifying disability plates or placards.

---

### Q630 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.006(a)

Under TC 681.006(a), a vehicle operated by or for the transportation of a person with a disability, displaying qualifying plates or a placard, may be parked in a designated disability parking space for:

- [x] An unlimited period
- [ ] Up to 2 hours
- [ ] Up to 24 hours
- [ ] Up to 4 hours, renewable once

**Explanation:** There is no time limit on parking in a designated disability space under these conditions.

---

### Q631 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.008(a)(2)

Under TC 681.008(a)(2), a vehicle may be parked for an unlimited period in a designated disability parking space if it displays license plates issued by another state that indicate on the face of the plates that the owner or operator is a ______ of the United States armed forces.

**Answer:** disabled veteran

**Explanation:** Out-of-state plates that identify the owner/operator as a disabled veteran qualify for the same unlimited-period disability parking privilege.

---

### Q632 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.008(a)(2)

Under TC 681.008(a)(2), out-of-state license plates qualify a vehicle for unlimited-period disability parking if the plates indicate on their face that the owner or operator is a:

- [x] Disabled veteran of the United States armed forces
- [ ] Retired law enforcement officer
- [ ] Purple Heart recipient only
- [ ] Active-duty service member

**Explanation:** The statute specifically covers out-of-state plates identifying a disabled veteran.

---

### Q633 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.010(a)-(b)

Under TC 681.010, a peace officer, a person designated by a political subdivision to enforce parking regulations, or a ______ officer commissioned under Occupations Code Chapter 1702 and employed by the owner of ______ property may file a charge for an offense under Chapter 681 at a designated parking space or area.

**Answer:** security | private

**Explanation:** Enforcement authority under Chapter 681 extends to commissioned private security officers employed by the property owner, not just peace officers and political-subdivision designees.

---

### Q634 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.010(b)

Under TC 681.010(b), in addition to peace officers and political-subdivision designees, who else may file a charge for a Chapter 681 offense at a designated parking space?

- [x] A security officer commissioned under Occupations Code Chapter 1702 and employed by the owner of the private property
- [ ] Any private citizen who witnesses the violation
- [ ] A licensed private investigator
- [ ] A tow truck operator under contract with the property owner

**Explanation:** Commissioned private security officers employed by the property owner have this enforcement authority under 681.010(b).

---

### Q635 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.0101(b)-(c)

Under TC 681.0101, a person appointed by a political subdivision to enforce Chapter 681 must complete a training program of at least ______ hours, and while performing these duties the person is not a peace officer and may not carry a ______.

**Answer:** four | weapon

**Explanation:** These appointed enforcers get a minimum four-hour training program but have no peace-officer authority and cannot carry a weapon while on duty.

---

### Q636 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.0101(b)

Under TC 681.0101(b), a person appointed by a political subdivision to enforce Chapter 681 must successfully complete a training program of at least how many hours?

- [x] 4 hours
- [ ] 2 hours
- [ ] 8 hours
- [ ] 40 hours

**Explanation:** Four hours is the statutory minimum training length for this appointed (non-peace-officer) enforcement role.

---

### Q637 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.011(e)

Under TC 681.011(e), in a prosecution under this section, it is presumed that the ______ of the motor vehicle is the person who left the vehicle standing at the time and place the offense occurred.

**Answer:** registered owner

**Explanation:** This presumption lets the prosecution proceed against the registered owner absent evidence someone else was driving.

---

### Q638 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.011(e)

Under TC 681.011(e), in a prosecution for an unlawful-disability-parking offense, it is presumed that the person who left the vehicle standing is:

- [x] The registered owner of the vehicle
- [ ] Whoever holds the disabled parking placard
- [ ] The person who reported the violation
- [ ] The last person ticketed for a Chapter 681 offense

**Explanation:** The registered owner is presumed to be the person who left the vehicle standing.

---

### Q639 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.012(a)

Under TC 681.012(a), an officer who seizes a disabled parking placard must, not later than ______ hours after the seizure, determine whether probable cause existed to believe that the offense was committed.

**Answer:** 48

**Explanation:** 48 hours is the deadline for the seizing officer to make the probable-cause determination; if none existed, the placard must be promptly returned.

---

### Q640 — Privileged Parking
**LO:** 22.23 · **Source:** TC 681.012(a)

Under TC 681.012(a), after seizing a disabled parking placard, an officer must determine whether probable cause existed for the seizure not later than:

- [x] 48 hours after the seizure
- [ ] 24 hours after the seizure
- [ ] 72 hours after the seizure
- [ ] 5 business days after the seizure

**Explanation:** 48 hours is the deadline; if the officer finds probable cause existed, the placard must be destroyed within 5 days and the department notified.

---

### Q641 — Abandoned Motor Vehicles
**LO:** 22.24 · **Source:** TC 683.002(a)(1)

Under TC 683.002(a)(1), a motor vehicle is abandoned if it is inoperable, is more than ______ years old, and has been left unattended on public property for more than 48 hours.

**Answer:** five

**Explanation:** 683.002(a)(1) combines three conditions — inoperable, more than 5 years old, and unattended on public property more than 48 hours — to count as abandoned under that specific prong.

---

### Q642 — Abandoned Motor Vehicles
**LO:** 22.24 · **Source:** TC 683.002(a)(1)

Under TC 683.002(a)(1), a vehicle qualifies as "abandoned" on the inoperable/age/public-property prong only if it is inoperable, more than how many years old, and left unattended on public property for more than 48 hours?

- [x] Five years
- [ ] Three years
- [ ] Ten years
- [ ] Two years

**Explanation:** All three conditions — inoperable, more than 5 years old, and 48+ hours unattended on public property — must be met together under this specific prong of 683.002(a).

---

### Q643 — Abandoned Motor Vehicles
**LO:** 22.24 · **Source:** TC 683.002(a)(3)-(4)

Under TC 683.002(a), a motor vehicle is also abandoned if it has remained on private property without the owner's consent for more than 48 hours, or has been left unattended on the right-of-way of a designated highway for more than ______ hours.

**Answer:** 48

**Explanation:** Both the private-property-without-consent prong (a)(3) and the highway-right-of-way prong (a)(4) use the same 48-hour threshold; only the turnpike-project prong (a)(5) uses a shorter 24-hour threshold.

---

### Q644 — Abandoned Motor Vehicles
**LO:** 22.24 · **Source:** TC 683.002(a)(5)

Under TC 683.002(a)(5), a vehicle left unattended on the right-of-way of a Texas Turnpike Authority project or a controlled-access highway is abandoned after how many hours — shorter than the general 48-hour rule?

- [x] 24 hours
- [ ] 12 hours
- [ ] 48 hours
- [ ] 72 hours

**Explanation:** Turnpike/controlled-access-highway rights-of-way get a shorter 24-hour abandonment threshold than the general 48-hour rule used elsewhere in 683.002(a).

---

### Q645 — Abandoned Motor Vehicles
**LO:** 22.24 · **Source:** TC 683.011(a)

Under TC 683.011(a), a law enforcement agency may take into custody an abandoned motor vehicle, aircraft, watercraft, or ______ found on public or private property.

**Answer:** outboard motor

**Explanation:** 683.011(a) extends the agency's custody authority beyond motor vehicles to aircraft, watercraft, and outboard motors as well.

---

### Q646 — Abandoned Motor Vehicles
**LO:** 22.24 · **Source:** TC 683.011(a)

Besides a motor vehicle, which of the following may a law enforcement agency take into custody under TC 683.011(a) if abandoned?

- [x] Aircraft, watercraft, or an outboard motor
- [ ] Only trailers and semitrailers
- [ ] Only commercial motor vehicles
- [ ] Farm equipment only

**Explanation:** 683.011(a) covers motor vehicles, aircraft, watercraft, and outboard motors found abandoned on public or private property.

---

### Q647 — Abandoned Motor Vehicles
**LO:** 22.24 · **Source:** TC 683.012(b)(1)

Under TC 683.012(b)(1), notice of an abandoned vehicle taken into custody must be sent by certified mail not later than the ______th day after the date the agency takes the vehicle into custody.

**Answer:** 10

**Explanation:** The agency has a 10-day window from taking custody (or from receiving a report under 683.031) to send certified-mail notice under 683.012(b)(1).

---

### Q648 — Abandoned Motor Vehicles
**LO:** 22.24 · **Source:** TC 683.012(b)(4)

Under TC 683.012(b)(4), the notice sent to an abandoned vehicle's owner and lienholder must inform them of the right to claim the vehicle not later than which day after the date of the notice?

- [x] The 20th day
- [ ] The 10th day
- [ ] The 30th day
- [ ] The 5th day

**Explanation:** 683.012(b)(4) gives the owner/lienholder 20 days from the date of notice to claim the vehicle on payment of towing, preservation, and storage charges.

---

### Q649 — Abandoned Motor Vehicles
**LO:** 22.24 · **Source:** TC 683.012(b)(5)

Under TC 683.012(b)(5), failure of the owner or lienholder to claim the vehicle within the notice period is a waiver of all right, title, and interest in the vehicle, and consent to its sale at a ______ auction.

**Answer:** public

**Explanation:** Failing to claim within the 20-day window under 683.012(b)(4)-(5) waives ownership rights and consents to disposal by public auction.

---

### Q650 — Abandoned Motor Vehicles
**LO:** 22.24 · **Source:** TC 683.012(c)

Under TC 683.012(c), notice by publication in a newspaper of general circulation is sufficient in place of certified mail when:

- [x] The identity of the last registered owner cannot be determined
- [ ] The vehicle is worth less than $500
- [ ] The vehicle was stolen
- [ ] The owner lives out of state

**Explanation:** 683.012(c) allows substituted notice by publication when the last registered owner's identity can't be determined (among other listed conditions in that subsection).

---

### Q651 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.001(7)

Under TC 502.001(7), a "commercial motor vehicle" means a motor vehicle, other than a motorcycle or moped, designed or used primarily to ______ property.

**Answer:** transport

**Explanation:** 502.001(7) defines "commercial motor vehicle" around transporting property, and specifically includes a passenger car reconstructed and used primarily for delivery purposes.

---

### Q652 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.001(7)

Under TC 502.001(7), which vehicle is expressly excluded from the definition of "commercial motor vehicle" even though it makes deliveries?

- [x] A passenger car used to deliver the United States mail
- [ ] A passenger car reconstructed for delivery purposes
- [ ] A pickup truck hauling tools
- [ ] A van used for a catering business

**Explanation:** 502.001(7) excludes a passenger car used to deliver U.S. mail, even though it otherwise includes reconstructed delivery passenger cars.

---

### Q653 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.001(11)

Under TC 502.001(11), "Department" means the Texas Department of ______ ______.

**Answer:** Motor Vehicles

**Explanation:** Throughout Chapter 502, "Department" refers to the Texas Department of Motor Vehicles (TxDMV), distinct from the Department of Public Safety.

---

### Q654 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.001(11)

Under TC 502.001(11), which agency is meant whenever Chapter 502 refers to "the department"?

- [x] The Texas Department of Motor Vehicles
- [ ] The Texas Department of Public Safety
- [ ] The Texas Department of Transportation
- [ ] The Texas Department of Insurance

**Explanation:** "Department" in Chapter 502 (registration of vehicles) means the Texas Department of Motor Vehicles, not DPS or TxDOT.

---

### Q655 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.001(15)

Under TC 502.001(15), a "farm semitrailer" or "farm trailer" means a vehicle designed and used primarily as a ______ vehicle.

**Answer:** farm

**Explanation:** The definition is circular by design — it just confirms the vehicle's primary designed/used purpose must be farming.

---

### Q656 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.001(16)

Under TC 502.001(16), where does the definition of "farm tractor" for registration purposes come from?

- [x] It has the meaning assigned by Section 541.201
- [ ] It has its own standalone definition in 502.001
- [ ] It has the meaning assigned by Section 502.146
- [ ] It has the meaning assigned by Section 621.001

**Explanation:** 502.001(16) borrows the "farm tractor" definition from 541.201 (the general vehicle-and-person definitions section) rather than defining it independently.

---

### Q657 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.001(20)

Under TC 502.001(20), "implements of husbandry" has the meaning assigned by Section ______.

**Answer:** 541.201

**Explanation:** Like "farm tractor," "implements of husbandry" is a borrowed definition pointing back to the general definitions in 541.201.

---

### Q658 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.001(30)

Under TC 502.001(30), "operate temporarily on the highways" describes travel between which of the following?

- [x] Different farms, a place of supply/storage and a farm, or an owner's farm and where farm produce is marketed
- [ ] Any two points within the same county
- [ ] A dealership and a customer's home
- [ ] A weigh station and a highway on-ramp

**Explanation:** 502.001(30) lists three specific farm-related travel patterns that qualify as "operating temporarily on the highways" for exemption purposes.

---

### Q659 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.001(33)

Under TC 502.001(33), a "power sweeper" is an implement designed for the removal of debris, dirt, gravel, litter, or sand from asphaltic or cement concrete surfaces using a broom, vacuum, or ______ air system.

**Answer:** regenerative

**Explanation:** 502.001(33) lists three removal mechanisms for a power sweeper: broom, vacuum, or regenerative air system.

---

### Q660 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.001(36)

Under TC 502.001(36), "public property" means property that is:

- [x] Owned or leased by this state or a political subdivision of this state
- [ ] Open to the public regardless of ownership
- [ ] Owned by any government, state or federal
- [ ] Any land not fenced or posted as private

**Explanation:** 502.001(36) ties "public property" specifically to ownership or lease by the state or one of its political subdivisions — not mere public accessibility.

---

### Q661 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.005(b)

Under TC 502.005(b), for purposes of registering a vehicle under Chapter 502, an autocycle is considered to be a ______.

**Answer:** motorcycle

**Explanation:** Even though an autocycle has up to three wheels and doesn't require straddling a seat, 502.005(b) treats it as a motorcycle for registration purposes.

---

### Q662 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.040(a)

Under TC 502.040(a), the owner of a vehicle must apply for registration not more than how many days after purchasing the vehicle or becoming a Texas resident?

- [x] 30 days
- [ ] 10 days
- [ ] 60 days
- [ ] 90 days

**Explanation:** 502.040(a) sets the general registration deadline at 30 days from purchase or from becoming a Texas resident.

---

### Q663 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.046(a)

Under TC 502.046(a), a county assessor-collector may not register a motor vehicle unless the owner submits evidence of ______ ______ required by Section 601.051.

**Answer:** financial responsibility

**Explanation:** 502.046 ties vehicle registration directly to proof of financial responsibility (insurance or an approved alternative) under Chapter 601.

---

### Q664 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.059(c)

Under TC 502.059(c), where must the registration insignia validating a license plate be attached on a vehicle with a windshield?

- [x] Inside the windshield, lower left corner, without obstructing the driver's vision
- [ ] On the rear bumper
- [ ] Inside the windshield, lower right corner
- [ ] On the front license plate only

**Explanation:** 502.059(c) requires the insignia in the lower left corner of the inside of the windshield (with a separate rule for vehicles without a windshield, like motorcycles).

---

### Q665 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.094(c)(2)

Under TC 502.094(c)(2), the department may issue a temporary registration permit for a fee of $25 for a 72-hour permit or $______ for a 144-hour permit.

**Answer:** 50

**Explanation:** The 144-hour (6-day) permit costs double the 72-hour (3-day) permit — $50 versus $25 — under 502.094(c)(2).

---

### Q666 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.095(c)-(d)

Under TC 502.095, a one-trip license plate may not be valid for longer than how many days, while a 30-day license plate covers a longer, separately defined period?

- [x] 15 days for the one-trip plate
- [ ] 30 days for the one-trip plate
- [ ] 7 days for the one-trip plate
- [ ] 45 days for the one-trip plate

**Explanation:** 502.095(c) caps a one-trip plate at 15 days from the effective date of registration, distinct from the separate 30-day plate under (d).

---

### Q667 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.140(b)

Under TC 502.140(b), except as otherwise provided, the department may not register an ______-______ vehicle for operation on a public highway.

**Answer:** off-highway

**Explanation:** 502.140 generally bars registering an off-highway vehicle (as defined in Ch.551A) for public-highway use, with a narrow exception for government-owned OHVs used for public safety on beaches/highways.

---

### Q668 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.142

Under TC 502.142, manufactured housing is treated how for purposes of Chapter 502?

- [x] It is not a vehicle subject to the chapter at all
- [ ] It must be registered as a trailer
- [ ] It must be registered as a semitrailer
- [ ] It is exempt only if towed under 45 feet

**Explanation:** 502.142 simply excludes manufactured housing (as defined in the Occupations Code) from being a "vehicle" subject to Chapter 502's registration scheme.

---

### Q669 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.143

Under TC 502.143, an owner may not register power sweepers, motorized mobility devices, electric personal assistive mobility devices, or ______ ______ for operation on a public highway.

**Answer:** electric bicycles

**Explanation:** 502.143 lists four device types that simply cannot be registered for public-highway operation, including electric bicycles as defined by 664.001.

---

### Q670 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.144

Under TC 502.144, crossing a public highway that separates real property under the vehicle owner's control is treated as:

- [x] Not a "use" of the motor vehicle on the public highway at all
- [ ] A use requiring a 72-hour permit
- [ ] A use requiring full registration
- [ ] A use only exempt for farm tractors

**Explanation:** 502.144 carves this specific crossing scenario out of the definition of "use on the public highway" entirely, regardless of vehicle type.

---

### Q671 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.146(b)(1)

Under TC 502.146(b)(1), an owner is not required to register a farm trailer or farm semitrailer with a gross weight of more than 4,000 pounds but not more than ______ pounds used exclusively for specified farm hauling.

**Answer:** 34,000

**Explanation:** The registration exemption in 502.146(b)(1) applies to a specific weight band — over 4,000 up to 34,000 pounds — for qualifying farm trailers/semitrailers.

---

### Q672 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.433(a)

Under TC 502.433(a), the registration fee for a commercial motor vehicle registered as a farm vehicle is what percentage of the applicable standard fee?

- [x] 50 percent
- [ ] 25 percent
- [ ] 75 percent
- [ ] 100 percent, no discount

**Explanation:** 502.433(a) gives qualifying farm-use commercial motor vehicles a 50% discount off the standard registration fee under 502.252 or 502.253.

---

### Q673 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.407(a)

Under TC 502.407(a), it is an offense to operate a vehicle displaying an expired license plate after the ______th working day after the registration expires, if the plate hasn't been validated for the current period.

**Answer:** fifth

**Explanation:** 502.407(a) builds in a 5-working-day grace period after expiration before displaying the old plate becomes an offense.

---

### Q674 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.471(c)

Under TC 502.471(c) — the Chapter 502 general penalty catchall — an offense under this section is a misdemeanor punishable by a fine not to exceed:

- [x] $200
- [ ] $500
- [ ] $1,000
- [ ] $2,000

**Explanation:** 502.471(c) sets the registration chapter's general penalty at a fine up to $200 when no other specific penalty applies (subject to the exclusions in 502.471(b)).

---

### Q675 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.472

Under TC 502.472, a person commits an offense if the person operates a motor vehicle that has not been registered, or registered for a class ______ than that to which the vehicle belongs.

**Answer:** other

**Explanation:** 502.472 covers both no registration and registration in the wrong vehicle class.

---

### Q676 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.473(a)-(b)

Under TC 502.473, operating without a properly displayed registration insignia is an offense both for ordinary motor vehicles and, under a separate subsection, for which group of vehicles?

- [x] Road tractors, motorcycles, trailers, or semitrailers
- [ ] Farm tractors only
- [ ] Golf carts and NEVs only
- [ ] Buses only

**Explanation:** 502.473(a) covers motor vehicles generally, while (b) separately calls out road tractors, motorcycles, trailers, and semitrailers, which display insignia differently.

---

### Q677 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.474

Under TC 502.474, a person commits an offense if the person operates a vehicle for which a one-trip permit is required without the registration receipt and properly displayed temporary ______.

**Answer:** tag

**Explanation:** 502.474 requires both the receipt and a properly displayed temporary tag for a one-trip-permit vehicle.

---

### Q678 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.475(b), (d)

Under TC 502.475, displaying a registration insignia that is knowingly altered to make identification marks illegible is punished as what level of offense — worse than the base $200 fine?

- [x] Class B misdemeanor
- [ ] Class A misdemeanor
- [ ] Class C misdemeanor
- [ ] State jail felony

**Explanation:** The base offense under 502.475(a) is a fine up to $200, but knowing alteration to make marks illegible (or a fictitious insignia under (d)) escalates it to a Class B misdemeanor.

---

### Q679 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.4755(e)

Under TC 502.4755(e), manufacturing or selling a deceptively similar registration insignia is a felony of the ______ degree.

**Answer:** third

**Explanation:** 502.4755(e)(1) makes manufacturing/selling a state-jail-adjacent third-degree felony, while mere possession under (e)(2) is only a Class C misdemeanor (Class B if a prior conviction).

---

### Q680 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.491(a)

Under TC 502.491(a), on the sale or transfer of a vehicle, what happens to the registration insignia and the remaining registration period?

- [x] The insignia must be removed, and the remaining registration period expires at the time of sale or transfer
- [ ] The insignia stays with the vehicle automatically
- [ ] The registration period transfers to the new owner unchanged
- [ ] Nothing changes until the next renewal date

**Explanation:** 502.491(a) terminates the remaining registration period at the moment of sale/transfer and requires removal of the insignia.

---

### Q681 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.492(d)

Under TC 502.492(d), a temporary transit permit for a purchased vehicle may not be valid for longer than a ______-day period.

**Answer:** five

**Explanation:** 502.492(d) caps the temporary transit permit (used when plates/insignia have been removed under 502.491) at 5 days.

---

### Q682 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 503.061(a)

Under TC 503.061(a), instead of registering under Chapter 502, a dealer may apply for dealer's license plates if it is a vehicle the dealer sells and one for which the dealer has been issued a general ______ number.

**Answer:** distinguishing

**Explanation:** A dealer's "general distinguishing number" (GDN) is the license issued under Chapter 503 that dealer plates and other dealer privileges hinge on.

---

### Q683 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 503.062(a)

Under TC 503.062(a), a dealer's temporary license plate may be issued for use on an unregistered vehicle for which of these purposes?

- [x] Demonstrating the vehicle to a prospective buyer, conveying it between locations, or road testing it
- [ ] Permanent personal use by the dealer's family
- [ ] Renting the vehicle out long-term
- [ ] Use on any vehicle not in the dealer's inventory

**Explanation:** 503.062(a) lists specific dealer-business purposes — demonstration, conveyance between locations/repair, road testing, or charitable use — not general personal or rental use.

---

### Q684 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 503.063(a)

Under TC 503.063(a), except as otherwise provided, a dealer shall issue to a person who buys a vehicle a license plate or set of license plates that is valid while the ______ application submitted on the buyer's behalf is pending.

**Answer:** registration

**Explanation:** 503.063 bridges the gap between purchase and permanent registration by requiring the dealer to issue plates valid during the pending registration application.

---

### Q685 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 503.0631(a)

Under TC 503.0631(a), the department must develop and maintain what kind of system for dealer-issued license plates?

- [x] A secure, real-time database usable by law enforcement like vehicle registration information
- [ ] A paper filing system updated annually
- [ ] A public website open to any visitor
- [ ] No system is required — only the dealer keeps records

**Explanation:** 503.0631(a) requires a secure, real-time database so law enforcement can look up dealer-issued plate information the same way they check regular registration.

---

### Q686 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 503.068(b)

Under TC 503.068(b), a person may not use a metal dealer's license plate on a service or work vehicle (with a narrow exception) or on a commercial vehicle that is carrying a ______.

**Answer:** load

**Explanation:** 503.068(b) bars dealer plates on loaded commercial vehicles and, with a limited exception in (b-1), on service/work vehicles.

---

### Q687 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 503.070(a)-(b)

Under TC 503.070, a dealer who purchases a vehicle displaying an out-of-state license plate must:

- [x] Remove the plate within a reasonable time, and may not operate the vehicle for resale while it still displays that plate
- [ ] Keep the plate attached until sale
- [ ] Immediately destroy the plate on-site
- [ ] Mail the plate back to the other state within 24 hours

**Explanation:** 503.070(a) requires reasonably prompt removal, and (b) separately bars operating the vehicle for resale purposes while the out-of-state plate is still displayed.

---

### Q688 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 503.094(b)

Under TC 503.094(b), except as otherwise provided, a violation of Chapter 503 is a misdemeanor punishable by a fine of not less than $50 or more than $______.

**Answer:** 5,000

**Explanation:** 503.094(b)'s general penalty range ($50-$5,000) is far wider than the flat $200-ish penalties common in Chapter 502 — dealer-licensing violations carry more financial exposure.

---

### Q689 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 504.901(a)-(b)

Under TC 504.901, who is responsible for removing a vehicle's license plates on a sale or transfer?

- [x] The dealer (if sold to a GDN-holding dealer) or the seller (if sold to a non-dealer)
- [ ] Always the buyer, regardless of who they are
- [ ] The county tax assessor-collector
- [ ] TxDMV headquarters by mail request

**Explanation:** 504.901(a) puts removal on the dealer when selling to another GDN holder; (b) puts it on the seller when the buyer doesn't hold a GDN.

---

### Q690 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 504.941(a)

Under TC 504.941(a), violating the antique-vehicle restriction in Section 504.502 is a misdemeanor punishable by a fine of not less than $5 or more than $______.

**Answer:** 200

**Explanation:** 504.941(a)'s fine range ($5-$200) is one of the narrowest/lowest in the license-plate offense sections.

---

### Q691 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 504.943(a)-(b)

Under TC 504.943, operating without a required license plate is split into two subsections based on:

- [x] Whether the vehicle needs two plates (Subsection (a)) or just one, like a motorcycle or trailer (Subsection (b))
- [ ] Whether the offense occurred on a state highway or a city street
- [ ] Whether the vehicle is registered in Texas or another state
- [ ] Whether the driver has a prior conviction

**Explanation:** 504.943(a) covers standard two-plate vehicles; (b) separately covers road tractors, motorcycles, trailers, and semitrailers, which only display one plate.

---

### Q692 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 504.944

Under TC 504.944, it is an offense to operate, or as owner permit another to operate, a motor vehicle displaying a number plate or registration insignia issued for a ______ vehicle.

**Answer:** different

**Explanation:** 504.944 is the "wrong plate on the wrong car" offense — attaching plates/insignia validly issued, but for a different vehicle.

---

### Q693 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 504.945(a)(5)-(7)

Under TC 504.945, besides a fictitious or reassigned plate, which of the following also counts as a "wrong, fictitious, altered, or obscured" license plate violation?

- [x] Blurring, an unauthorized attached device, or a coating that impairs readability of the plate or state name
- [ ] A plate that is simply dirty from normal driving
- [ ] A plate frame sold by the dealership
- [ ] A plate that is slightly bent from a minor accident

**Explanation:** 504.945(a)(5)-(7) extend the offense to physical readability impairments — blurring/reflective matter, unauthorized attachments, and coverings/coatings — not just swapped or fake plates.

---

### Q694 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 504.946(e)

Under TC 504.946(e), manufacturing or selling a deceptively similar license plate is a felony of the ______ degree — mirroring the registration-insignia version of this offense.

**Answer:** third

**Explanation:** 504.946(e)(1) matches 502.4755(e)(1) exactly: manufacture/sale of a deceptively similar plate/insignia is a third-degree felony, while mere possession is a lesser misdemeanor.

---

### Q695 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 504.9465(b)-(c)

Under TC 504.9465, what distinguishes purchasing/possessing a "license plate flipper" from manufacturing or distributing one?

- [x] Purchase/possession with criminal negligence is a Class B misdemeanor; manufacture/sale/distribution is a Class A misdemeanor
- [ ] Both are treated identically as Class C misdemeanors
- [ ] Purchase/possession is a felony; manufacture is a misdemeanor
- [ ] Only manufacturing is criminalized at all

**Explanation:** 504.9465(b) grades simple purchase/possession as Class B, while (c) grades manufacturing, selling, or distributing a plate flipper one level higher, as Class A.

---

### Q696 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.001

Transportation Code Chapter 501 may be cited as the ______.

**Answer:** Certificate of Title Act

**Explanation:** TC 501.001 gives Chapter 501 its short title: the Certificate of Title Act.

---

### Q697 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.003

Chapter 501, the Certificate of Title Act, is to be liberally construed to lessen and prevent which of the following?

- [x] Motor vehicle theft, trafficking in stolen vehicles, and the sale of an encumbered vehicle without disclosing the lien to the purchaser
- [ ] Unsafe vehicle equipment being operated on public highways
- [ ] Uninsured drivers operating without financial responsibility
- [ ] Unlicensed dealers issuing their own license plates

**Explanation:** 501.003 states the chapter's purpose: preventing vehicle theft, trafficking in stolen vehicles, and undisclosed liens on vehicles sold to a purchaser.

---

### Q698 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.042

Under TC 502.042, the department may not register or renew the registration of a motor vehicle for which a title is required unless the owner holds a title issued under Transportation Code Chapter ______.

**Answer:** 501

**Explanation:** Registration (Ch.502) and titling (Ch.501) are linked — 502.042 makes a Chapter 501 title (or an application for one) a prerequisite to registering a vehicle that requires one.

---

### Q699 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 502.042

Under TC 502.042, the Department of Motor Vehicles may not register or renew registration of a vehicle that requires a title unless the owner:

- [x] Holds a title issued under Chapter 501, or has applied for one
- [ ] Has passed a state safety inspection within the last 90 days
- [ ] Has proof of liability insurance dated within the last 30 days
- [ ] Has paid all outstanding parking citations in the county of registration

**Explanation:** 502.042 conditions registration/renewal on the owner holding (or having applied for) a Chapter 501 certificate of title.

---

### Q700 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.091(9)

Under TC 501.091(9), a "nonrepairable motor vehicle" is one that is damaged, wrecked, or burned to the extent that its only residual value is as a source of ______ or scrap metal.

**Answer:** parts

**Explanation:** 501.091(9)(A) defines a nonrepairable motor vehicle by a total-loss threshold: damage so severe the vehicle is worth something only for parts or scrap, not repair.

---

### Q701 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.091(9), (15)

Which of the following best distinguishes a "nonrepairable motor vehicle" from a "salvage motor vehicle" under TC 501.091?

- [x] A nonrepairable vehicle's only residual value is parts/scrap; a salvage vehicle's repair cost merely exceeds its actual cash value, so it may still be economically repaired and re-titled
- [ ] A nonrepairable vehicle is always foreign-made; a salvage vehicle is always domestic
- [ ] A nonrepairable vehicle has a lien on it; a salvage vehicle does not
- [ ] There is no legal distinction — the two terms are interchangeable under Chapter 501

**Explanation:** "Nonrepairable" means damage beyond any repair value (parts/scrap only); "salvage" means damage or a missing major component part where repair cost exceeds the vehicle's pre-damage actual cash value — a lower bar, since a salvage vehicle can still be rebuilt and issued a regular title later under 501.100.

---

### Q702 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.072(a)

Under TC 501.072(a), the transferor of a motor vehicle must provide the transferee a disclosure of the vehicle's ______ reading at the time of transfer.

**Answer:** odometer

**Explanation:** 501.072(a) requires an odometer disclosure statement at the time of transfer, in compliance with the federal odometer disclosure law.

---

### Q703 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.072(a)

Under TC 501.072(a), a person who sells a motor vehicle in Texas must provide the buyer with:

- [x] A disclosure of the vehicle's odometer reading at the time of transfer
- [ ] A certified copy of the vehicle's original manufacturer invoice
- [ ] A written 30-day mechanical warranty
- [ ] A notarized statement of the vehicle's accident history

**Explanation:** 501.072(a) requires the odometer-reading disclosure at transfer; it does not require a warranty, invoice copy, or accident-history statement.

---

### Q704 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.109(d)-(e)

Under TC 501.109, the salvage/nonrepairable title offenses in that section (such as applying for a title on a rebuilt nonrepairable vehicle) are a Class C misdemeanor on a first offense, a Class B misdemeanor with one prior conviction, and a ______ with two or more prior convictions.

**Answer:** state jail felony

**Explanation:** 501.109(d)-(e) escalates the baseline Class C misdemeanor for these offenses to Class B on one prior conviction and to a state jail felony on two or more.

---

### Q705 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.109(g)-(h)

Under TC 501.109(g), knowingly providing false information or forging another person's signature on an application, assignment, or lien-discharge document for a nonrepairable or salvage motor vehicle title is classified as:

- [x] Felony, 3rd degree
- [ ] Class A misdemeanor
- [ ] Class C misdemeanor
- [ ] State jail felony

**Explanation:** 501.109(h) sets this false-information/forgery offense at a third-degree felony — a harsher, standalone tier separate from the escalating Class C→B→state-jail-felony ladder that applies to the chapter's other salvage/nonrepairable title offenses.

---

### Q706 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.151

Under TC 501.151, a person commits an offense if they stamp or place a serial number on a vehicle or vehicle part with the intent of changing the vehicle's ______, and the offense is a felony of the third degree.

**Answer:** identity

**Explanation:** 501.151 targets VIN-switching — restamping a vehicle's serial number to disguise its true identity — as a third-degree felony, subject to a narrow affirmative defense for manufacturer or department employees acting within their official duties.

---

### Q707 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.151

Placing an altered serial number on a vehicle with the intent to change its identity — commonly done to disguise a stolen vehicle — is classified under TC 501.151 as:

- [x] Felony, 3rd degree
- [ ] Class A misdemeanor
- [ ] State jail felony
- [ ] Felony, 2nd degree

**Explanation:** TC 501.151 makes this VIN-alteration offense a third-degree felony.

---

### Q708 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.155

Under TC 501.155, knowingly providing false information or, without legal authority, signing another person's name on a title application, assignment, or lien-discharge document is a felony of the ______ degree.

**Answer:** third

**Explanation:** 501.155 makes title-document forgery/false-information a third-degree felony — the general-title-fraud counterpart to the narrower 501.109(g)-(h) offense that applies specifically to salvage/nonrepairable title documents.

---

### Q709 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.157(a)

Under TC 501.157(a), the general penalty that applies to a Chapter 501 offense with no penalty stated elsewhere in the chapter is a misdemeanor punishable by a fine of:

- [x] $1–$100 for a first offense, $2–$200 for a subsequent offense
- [ ] Up to $200, no jail — the standard Transportation Code catchall fine
- [ ] $175–$350 for a first offense
- [ ] Up to $500 and/or up to 6 months jail

**Explanation:** 501.157(a) is Chapter 501's own catchall penalty — a notably smaller fine range ($1–$100 first offense, $2–$200 subsequent) than the more familiar $200 catchalls used elsewhere in the Transportation Code (like 542.401 or 502.471).

---

### Q710 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.158

Under TC 501.158, a peace officer may seize a vehicle or vehicle part without a warrant if the officer has probable cause to believe the vehicle or part is stolen, or has had its serial number removed, altered, or ______.

**Answer:** obliterated

**Explanation:** 501.158 gives a peace officer independent, warrantless seizure authority — based on probable cause alone — for a vehicle or part that is stolen or that shows signs of serial-number tampering; the seized item may then be handled as stolen property for custody and disposition purposes.

---

### Q711 — Vehicle Registration & Titling
**LO:** 22.25 · **Source:** TC 501.158

An officer runs a VIN during a traffic stop and has probable cause to believe the plate on the vehicle's dash has been ground off and re-stamped. Under TC 501.158, what may the officer do?

- [x] Seize the vehicle or the affected part without a warrant, based on probable cause alone
- [ ] Nothing — a warrant is required before seizing any part of a validly-registered vehicle
- [ ] Only issue a citation; seizure requires the vehicle owner's consent
- [ ] Only detain the driver; the vehicle itself cannot be seized without a court order

**Explanation:** 501.158 authorizes warrantless seizure of a vehicle or part on probable cause that it is stolen or has an altered/removed/obliterated serial number — exactly the scenario of a ground-off and re-stamped VIN plate.

---

### Q712 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.051

Under TC 601.051, a person may not operate a motor vehicle in Texas unless financial responsibility is established through a liability insurance policy, a surety bond, a deposit with the comptroller, a deposit with the county judge, or ______-insurance.

**Answer:** self

**Explanation:** 601.051 lists five approved methods of establishing financial responsibility; self-insurance (601.124) is the fifth and requires more than 25 registered vehicles.

---

### Q713 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.051

Under TC 601.051, which of the following is NOT one of the five listed ways to establish financial responsibility for a motor vehicle?

- [x] A personal cash savings account of any amount
- [ ] A surety bond filed under Section 601.121
- [ ] A deposit with the comptroller under Section 601.122
- [ ] Self-insurance under Section 601.124

**Explanation:** The five methods are a liability policy, a surety bond, a deposit with the comptroller, a deposit with the county judge, or self-insurance — a generic savings account isn't one of them.

---

### Q714 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.052(a)(1)

Under TC 601.052(a)(1), the financial-responsibility requirement does not apply to a vehicle that is a former military vehicle or is at least ______ years old, used only for exhibitions/club activities/parades, with an owner's affidavit on file.

**Answer:** 25

**Explanation:** The collector-vehicle exception in 601.052(a)(1) requires both the 25-year/former-military-vehicle threshold and the limited-use affidavit.

---

### Q715 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.052(a)(2)-(2b)

Under TC 601.052, which of the following vehicles is exempt from the financial responsibility requirement, so long as it's operated only within its own authorized-use statute?

- [x] A neighborhood electric vehicle, golf cart, or off-highway vehicle
- [ ] Any commercial motor vehicle
- [ ] Any vehicle under $5,000 in value
- [ ] Any vehicle more than 10 years old

**Explanation:** 601.052(a)(2), (2-a), and (2-b) exempt NEVs, golf carts, and OHVs respectively, but only when operated within the bounds their own authorizing statutes allow.

---

### Q716 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.053(a)

Under TC 601.053(a), on request by a peace officer or a person involved in a collision, the operator must provide evidence of financial responsibility — one accepted form is a standard proof of motor vehicle liability insurance form prescribed by the Texas Department of ______.

**Answer:** Insurance

**Explanation:** 601.053(a)(2) references the standard proof-of-insurance form, which the Texas Department of Insurance (not TxDMV or DPS) prescribes under 601.081.

---

### Q717 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.054(a)

Under TC 601.054(a), the department must accept an owner's evidence of financial responsibility as a substitute for which other person's evidence?

- [x] An operator employed by the owner, or a member of the owner's immediate family/household
- [ ] Any stranger who borrows the vehicle once
- [ ] A rental car company's customer
- [ ] A repossession agent

**Explanation:** 601.054(a) limits the substitution to employees of the owner or immediate family/household members — not any borrower.

---

### Q718 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.071(1)

Under TC 601.071(1), a motor vehicle liability insurance policy must be issued by an insurance company ______ to write motor vehicle liability insurance in Texas.

**Answer:** authorized

**Explanation:** 601.071(1) requires the issuing company to be authorized in Texas (with a narrow exception referenced in 601.083).

---

### Q719 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.073(a)

Under TC 601.073(a), which of the following must a motor vehicle liability insurance policy state?

- [x] The named insured's name/address, coverage provided, premium charged, policy period, and limits of liability
- [ ] Only the premium and nothing else
- [ ] Only the policy period
- [ ] The insured's driving record

**Explanation:** 601.073(a) lists five required disclosures on the face of the policy — name/address, coverage, premium, period, and liability limits.

---

### Q720 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.081(b)

Under TC 601.081(b), a standard proof of motor vehicle liability insurance form must include the name of the insurer, the policy number, the policy period, the name/address of each insured, the policy limits (or a compliance statement), and the ______ and ______ of each covered vehicle.

**Answer:** make | model

**Explanation:** 601.081(b) requires six categories of information on the standard proof form, ending with the make and model of each covered vehicle.

---

### Q721 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.121(a)

Under TC 601.121(a), a surety bond used to establish financial responsibility must have at least how many individual sureties, each owning non-exempt real property in Texas?

- [x] Two
- [ ] One
- [ ] Three
- [ ] Four

**Explanation:** 601.121(a)(1) requires at least two individual sureties, each holding qualifying real property, and the bond must not be cancelable for at least 6 days after notice.

---

### Q722 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.122(a)

Under TC 601.122(a), a person may establish financial responsibility by depositing $______ with the comptroller, in cash or qualifying securities.

**Answer:** 55,000

**Explanation:** Both the comptroller deposit (601.122) and the county judge deposit (601.123) use the same $55,000 figure as the cash/securities alternative to insurance.

---

### Q723 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.123(a)-(b)

Under TC 601.123, a cash-or-cashier's-check deposit establishing financial responsibility is made with which official, in what minimum amount?

- [x] The county judge of the county where the vehicle is registered, at least $55,000
- [ ] The comptroller, at least $25,000
- [ ] The county sheriff, at least $10,000
- [ ] The county judge, at least $100,000

**Explanation:** 601.123(a)-(b) requires the deposit with the county judge of the county of registration, in the same $55,000 minimum used for the comptroller-deposit alternative.

---

### Q724 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.124(a)

Under TC 601.124(a), a person in whose name more than ______ motor vehicles are registered may qualify as a self-insurer.

**Answer:** 25

**Explanation:** The self-insurance option under 601.124 is only available to owners of large fleets — more than 25 registered vehicles.

---

### Q725 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.191(b)-(c)

Under TC 601.191, operating a motor vehicle without required liability insurance is punishable by a fine of $175-$350 for a first offense, rising to what range for a person previously convicted under this section?

- [x] $350 to $1,000
- [ ] $500 to $1,500
- [ ] $175 to $700
- [ ] $1,000 to $2,000

**Explanation:** 601.191(c) roughly doubles-to-triples both ends of the fine range for a repeat offender compared to the first-offense range in (b).

---

### Q726 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.193(a)

Under TC 601.193(a), it is a defense to prosecution under 601.191 or 601.195 that the person produces to the court one of the documents listed in Section 601.053(a) that was valid at the time of the alleged ______.

**Answer:** offense

**Explanation:** This is the "I actually had insurance" defense — producing a valid proof-of-financial-responsibility document from the time of the alleged offense requires dismissal once verified.

---

### Q727 — Financial Responsibility & Insurance
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 601.194

Under TC 601.194, it is a defense to a 601.191 prosecution that the vehicle operated by the defendant was:

- [x] In the defendant's possession solely for maintenance or repair, and not owned in whole or part by the defendant
- [ ] Borrowed from a friend for a road trip
- [ ] A rental car with the company's own insurance
- [ ] Uninsured but under $1,000 in value

**Explanation:** 601.194's defense is narrow — it only covers someone who has the vehicle purely for maintenance/repair purposes and has no ownership interest in it.

---

### Q728 — Vehicle Size & Weight
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 621.101(a)(1)-(2)

Under TC 621.101(a), a vehicle may not have a single axle weight heavier than 20,000 pounds or a tandem axle weight heavier than ______ pounds, including enforcement tolerances.

**Answer:** 34,000

**Explanation:** 621.101(a)(1)-(2) sets the two most commonly cited axle-weight maximums: 20,000 lb single axle and 34,000 lb tandem axle.

---

### Q729 — Vehicle Size & Weight
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 621.201(a)-(b)

Under TC 621.201, the general maximum vehicle width on a public highway is 102 inches, but a passenger vehicle and its load are instead limited to:

- [x] Eight feet
- [ ] Ten feet
- [ ] 96 inches
- [ ] 120 inches

**Explanation:** 621.201(a) sets the general 102-inch limit, while (b) separately caps a passenger vehicle and its load at 8 feet (which happens to be slightly less than 102 inches).

---

### Q730 — Vehicle Size & Weight
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 621.203(a)

Under TC 621.203(a), a motor vehicle, other than a truck-tractor, may not be longer than ______ feet.

**Answer:** 45

**Explanation:** 621.203(a)'s 45-foot cap has a specific carve-out in (b) for certain longer motor buses equipped with air brakes and extra axles/tires.

---

### Q731 — Vehicle Size & Weight
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 621.204(a)-(b)

Under TC 621.204, a semitrailer operated in a truck-tractor/semitrailer combination may not exceed 59 feet, while a semitrailer or trailer in a three-vehicle combination is capped at:

- [x] 28-1/2 feet
- [ ] 35 feet
- [ ] 45 feet
- [ ] 65 feet

**Explanation:** 621.204(a) sets 59 feet for the simpler two-vehicle combination, and (b) sets a shorter 28.5-foot limit when a third vehicle (a trailer) is added to the combination.

---

### Q732 — Vehicle Size & Weight
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 621.205(a)

Under TC 621.205(a), a combination of not more than ______ vehicles may generally be coupled together, so long as the combination (other than a truck-tractor combination) is not longer than 65 feet.

**Answer:** three

**Explanation:** 621.205(a) caps the coupled-vehicle count at three (e.g., truck-tractor plus two trailers) and the overall combination length at 65 feet.

---

### Q733 — Vehicle Size & Weight
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 621.206(a)

Under TC 621.206(a), a vehicle or combination may not carry a load that extends more than three feet beyond its front, or (except as permitted by other law) more than how far beyond its rear?

- [x] Four feet
- [ ] Three feet
- [ ] Six feet
- [ ] Ten feet

**Explanation:** 621.206(a) sets an asymmetric extended-load limit: 3 feet at the front, 4 feet at the rear.

---

### Q734 — Vehicle Size & Weight
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 621.2061

Under TC 621.2061, a trailer may carry a load extending more than four feet beyond its rear — up to ______ feet — if the load is a motor vehicle designed to be carried there for loading/unloading a commodity.

**Answer:** seven

**Explanation:** 621.2061 creates a narrow exception to the standard 4-foot rear-overhang rule in 621.206, extending it to 7 feet for a specifically designed rear-mounted loading vehicle.

---

### Q735 — Vehicle Size & Weight
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 621.207(a)-(b)

Under TC 621.207, the absolute maximum height for a vehicle and its load is 14 feet, but an operator has an added duty once the vehicle exceeds what height?

- [x] 13 feet 6 inches — ensuring it clears every structure in its path
- [ ] 12 feet
- [ ] 14 feet exactly
- [ ] 10 feet

**Explanation:** 621.207(a) sets the flat 14-foot ceiling, while (b) imposes an extra duty on the operator once the vehicle passes 13'6" to confirm clearance through every structure ahead.

---

### Q736 — Vehicle Size & Weight
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 621.501(b)

Under TC 621.501(b), failing to carry or present a vehicle registration receipt as required by Section 621.002 is a misdemeanor punishable by a fine not to exceed $______.

**Answer:** 200

**Explanation:** 621.501(b) uses the same familiar $200 fine cap seen in many other Transportation Code catchall-style offenses.

---

### Q737 — Vehicle Size & Weight
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 621.504(b)-(c)

Under TC 621.504, striking a bridge or underpass due to excessive vehicle height is normally a Class C misdemeanor, but becomes a Class B misdemeanor (fine up to $500, up to 30 days jail, or both) if:

- [x] The person was not in compliance with all applicable license and permit requirements for operating the vehicle
- [ ] The structure was posted with a lower clearance than actual
- [ ] The vehicle was operated under police direction
- [ ] It was the driver's first offense

**Explanation:** 621.504(c) escalates the offense to Class B specifically when the driver also lacked proper licensing/permits — separate from the affirmative defenses in (d) for posted-clearance errors or police-directed movement.

---

### Q738 — Vehicle Size & Weight
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 622.901(b)

Under TC 622.901(b), the width limitation in Section 621.201 does not apply to highway building/maintenance machinery traveling during ______ on a public highway other than one in the interstate/defense highway system.

**Answer:** daylight

**Explanation:** 622.901(b)(1)(A) ties the width exception to daylight travel on non-interstate highways (with a separate limited allowance for up to 50 miles on an interstate).

---

### Q739 — Vehicle Size & Weight
**LO:** 22.26 · **Source:** TC 622.902(1)-(2)

Under TC 622.902, the length limitations in Sections 621.203-621.205 do not apply to which of the following?

- [x] Water-well drilling machinery, or a vehicle owned/operated by a fire department
- [ ] Any personally owned pickup truck
- [ ] Any vehicle registered as a farm vehicle
- [ ] School buses only

**Explanation:** 622.902 lists several specific exempt categories, including water-well drilling machinery (1) and fire department vehicles (2), among others like construction/pipeline equipment.

---

### Q740 — Warning Signs, Barricades & Highway Materials
**LO:** 22.27 · **Source:** TC 472.021(a)

Under TC 472.021(a), a person commits an offense if the person tampers with, damages, or removes a barricade, flare pot, sign, flasher signal, or other device warning of construction, repair, or ______ on or adjacent to a highway.

**Answer:** detour

**Explanation:** 472.021(a) — "Tampering with Warning Devices" — covers barricades, flare pots, signs, and flasher signals warning of construction, repair, or a detour.

---

### Q741 — Warning Signs, Barricades & Highway Materials
**LO:** 22.27 · **Source:** TC 472.021(c)

Under TC 472.021(c), tampering with a highway warning device is a misdemeanor punishable by a fine of not less than $25 or more than $1,000, confinement in county jail for a term not to exceed:

- [x] Two years
- [ ] One year
- [ ] 180 days
- [ ] Six months

**Explanation:** 472.021(c) allows up to two years in county jail — an unusually long county-jail term for a Transportation Code "misdemeanor," in addition to or instead of the fine.

---

### Q742 — Warning Signs, Barricades & Highway Materials
**LO:** 22.27 · **Source:** TC 472.021(b)

Under TC 472.021(b), the tampering offense does not apply to a person acting within the scope of employment as an officer, agent, contractor, employee, or ______ of the state, a political subdivision, a contractor, or a public utility.

**Answer:** trustee

**Explanation:** 472.021(b) exempts authorized workers — officers, agents, contractors, employees, or trustees acting within their job duties — from the tampering offense.

---

### Q743 — Warning Signs, Barricades & Highway Materials
**LO:** 22.27 · **Source:** TC 472.022(a)

Under TC 472.022(a), a person commits an offense if the person disobeys the instructions of a warning sign, or does what else?

- [x] Drives around a barricade
- [ ] Fails to signal a lane change near construction
- [ ] Parks within 500 feet of a work zone
- [ ] Photographs a construction site

**Explanation:** 472.022(a) creates two ways to commit this offense: disobeying a warning sign's instructions, or simply driving around a barricade.

---

### Q744 — Warning Signs, Barricades & Highway Materials
**LO:** 22.27 · **Source:** TC 472.022(d)(2)

Under TC 472.022(d)(2), disobeying a warning sign or barricade placed because water is over a road, street, or highway is a Class ______ misdemeanor — worse than the base fine-only penalty.

**Answer:** B

**Explanation:** 472.022(d) normally sets a $1-$200 fine, doubled to $2-$400 in an active work zone under (d)(1), but driving around a barricade placed for high water specifically escalates to a full Class B misdemeanor under (d)(2).

---

### Q745 — Warning Signs, Barricades & Highway Materials
**LO:** 22.27 · **Source:** TC 472.022(c)

Under TC 472.022(c), how does the statute treat multiple violations of this warning-sign section?

- [x] Each violation is a separate offense
- [ ] Only the first violation in a calendar year counts
- [ ] Violations merge into a single continuing offense
- [ ] Only violations causing a collision count

**Explanation:** 472.022(c) makes clear that each instance of disobeying a warning sign or driving around a barricade is charged as its own separate offense.

---

### Q746 — Warning Signs, Barricades & Highway Materials
**LO:** 22.28 · **Source:** TC 600.001(a)

Under TC 600.001(a), a person who drops or permits to be dropped or thrown on a highway destructive or injurious material shall ______ remove the material or cause it to be removed.

**Answer:** immediately

**Explanation:** 600.001(a) imposes an immediate cleanup duty, not a "reasonable time" standard, on whoever drops or allows destructive/injurious material onto a highway.

---

### Q747 — Warning Signs, Barricades & Highway Materials
**LO:** 22.28 · **Source:** TC 600.001(b)

Under TC 600.001(b), a person who removes a wrecked or damaged vehicle from a highway has a duty to also remove:

- [x] Glass or another injurious substance dropped on the highway from that vehicle
- [ ] Any nearby litter unrelated to the crash
- [ ] Skid marks left on the roadway
- [ ] Damaged guardrails

**Explanation:** 600.001(b) extends the cleanup duty specifically to glass and other injurious substances that came from the wrecked/damaged vehicle being removed.

---

### Q748 — Implied Consent
**LO:** 22.29 · **Source:** TC 724.011(a)

Under TC 724.011(a), a person arrested for an offense arising from operating a motor vehicle or watercraft while intoxicated is deemed to have ______ to submit to the taking of one or more specimens of breath or blood.

**Answer:** consented

**Explanation:** 724.011(a) is the core "implied consent" provision — arrest for an intoxication-related offense is deemed consent to a breath or blood specimen.

---

### Q749 — Implied Consent
**LO:** 22.29 · **Source:** TC 724.012(a-1)

Under TC 724.012(a-1), a peace officer SHALL require a blood specimen (a mandatory specimen) when the person is arrested for a Chapter 49 Penal Code offense, refuses to voluntarily submit, was the operator involved in a collision the officer believes resulted from the offense, and additionally:

- [x] The officer reasonably believes someone has died, will die, or suffered serious bodily injury as a direct result of the collision
- [ ] The person has a prior DWI conviction of any age
- [ ] The collision caused more than $500 in property damage
- [ ] A child under 15 was in the vehicle

**Explanation:** 724.012(a-1) requires all four conditions together — including the death/serious-bodily-injury element — before a mandatory blood draw is authorized under this subsection.

---

### Q750 — Implied Consent
**LO:** 22.29 · **Source:** TC 724.014(a)

Under TC 724.014(a), a person who is dead, unconscious, or otherwise incapable of refusal is considered not to have ______ the consent provided by Section 724.011.

**Answer:** withdrawn

**Explanation:** 724.014(a) means implied consent survives even if the person can't actively refuse — incapacity doesn't undo the consent already implied by 724.011.

---

### Q751 — Implied Consent
**LO:** 22.29 · **Source:** TC 724.015(a)(2)

Under TC 724.015(a)(2), before requesting a specimen, an officer must inform the person that refusal will result in automatic license suspension for not less than how many days?

- [x] 180 days
- [ ] 90 days
- [ ] 60 days
- [ ] 365 days

**Explanation:** 724.015(a)(2) sets the refusal-suspension warning at 180 days minimum — longer than the 90-day suspension warning for a 21-or-older person who submits and fails, under (a)(4).

---

### Q752 — Implied Consent
**LO:** 22.29 · **Source:** TC 724.016(a)

Under TC 724.016(a), a breath specimen must be taken and analyzed by an individual possessing a ______ issued by the department certifying the individual is qualified to perform the analysis.

**Answer:** certificate

**Explanation:** 724.016(a) requires the breath-test operator to hold a department-issued certificate of qualification; 724.016(c) lets the department revoke that certificate for cause.

---

### Q753 — Implied Consent
**LO:** 22.29 · **Source:** TC 724.017(a)

Under TC 724.017(a), which of the following is authorized to take a blood specimen at a peace officer's request or order?

- [x] A physician, qualified technician, registered nurse, licensed vocational nurse, or specially authorized EMT
- [ ] Any peace officer who has completed first-aid training
- [ ] Any hospital employee
- [ ] Only a licensed physician — no one else

**Explanation:** 724.017(a) lists five categories of qualified personnel, including certain EMTs authorized under the conditions in Subsection (c) — not just physicians.

---

### Q754 — Implied Consent
**LO:** 22.29 · **Source:** TC 724.018

Under TC 724.018, on request of a person who has given a specimen, full information concerning the analysis shall be made available to the person or the person's ______.

**Answer:** attorney

**Explanation:** 724.018 gives the tested person (or their attorney) a right to the full analysis results on request.

---

### Q755 — Implied Consent
**LO:** 22.29 · **Source:** TC 724.019(a)

Under TC 724.019(a), a person who submits to an officer-requested specimen may request an additional specimen taken by a professional of their own choosing, within what time limit after arrest?

- [x] Two hours
- [ ] One hour
- [ ] Four hours
- [ ] 24 hours

**Explanation:** 724.019(a) gives the arrested person up to 2 hours after arrest to arrange their own additional specimen for independent analysis.

---

### Q756 — Implied Consent
**LO:** 22.29 · **Source:** TC 724.031

Under TC 724.031, if a person refuses to submit to the taking of a specimen, the peace officer shall request the person to sign a ______ acknowledging the request, the warnings given, and the refusal.

**Answer:** statement

**Explanation:** 724.031 requires the officer to document refusal by asking the person to sign a statement covering all three elements: the request, the warning, and the refusal itself.

---

### Q757 — Implied Consent
**LO:** 22.29 · **Source:** TC 724.032(c)

Under TC 724.032(c), after a refusal, the officer must forward the notice of suspension/denial and the refusal report to the department not later than which business day after the arrest?

- [x] The fifth business day
- [ ] The second business day
- [ ] The tenth business day
- [ ] The next business day

**Explanation:** 724.032(c) gives the officer up to the 5th business day after arrest to forward the paperwork to the department.

---

### Q758 — Implied Consent
**LO:** 22.29 · **Source:** TC 724.002

Under TC 724.002, the license-suspension-for-refusal provisions apply only to a person arrested for an offense involving a motor vehicle or watercraft powered with an engine rated at ______ horsepower or above.

**Answer:** 50

**Explanation:** 724.002 limits the refusal/suspension scheme's applicability by engine size — 50 horsepower or above — mostly relevant for distinguishing small, unpowered, or low-powered watercraft.

---

### Q759 — Crash Investigation Procedures
**LO:** 22.30 · **Source:** TC 550.001(3)

Under TC 550.001, Chapter 550's duties-following-collision requirements apply to a highway or other public place, to certain roads owned by a water control and improvement district, and to a private access way or parking area provided for a client or patron by a ______ (other than a private residential property).

**Answer:** business

**Explanation:** 550.001 extends Chapter 550 beyond public highways to cover a business's customer access ways and parking areas, while excluding private residential property.

---

### Q760 — Crash Investigation Procedures
**LO:** 22.30 · **Source:** TC 550.041(a)

A collision occurs in a privately owned shopping-center parking lot (not a residential lot and no parking fee is charged), resulting in property damage of $1,500. Under TC 550.041, may a peace officer investigate and file justifiable charges?

- [x] Yes — 550.041 lets an officer investigate a collision causing injury, death, or at least $1,000 in apparent property damage regardless of whether Chapter 550 applies to that property
- [ ] No — the officer may only investigate collisions on a public highway
- [ ] No — private property collisions require the property owner's written consent first
- [ ] Yes, but only to issue a citation, never to file charges

**Explanation:** 550.041(a) authorizes a peace officer to investigate and file charges for a collision meeting the injury/death or $1,000+ property-damage threshold "without regard to whether the collision occurred on property to which this chapter applies" — the shopping-center lot qualifies since it isn't a private residential lot or a fee parking lot (the two carve-outs in 550.041(b)).

---

### Q761 — Crash Investigation Procedures
**LO:** 22.30 · **Source:** TC 550.062(a)-(b)

Under TC 550.062, an officer who investigates a collision resulting in injury, death, or at least $1,000 in property damage to one person's property must file a written collision report electronically with the department not later than the ______th day after the date of the collision.

**Answer:** 10

**Explanation:** 550.062(b) sets a 10-day deadline for the officer to electronically file the required collision report once the injury/death or $1,000 property-damage threshold is met.

---

### Q762 — Crash Investigation Procedures
**LO:** 22.30 · **Source:** TC 550.063

Under TC 550.063, who must approve the form used for a written collision report?

- [x] The Texas Department of Transportation and the Department of Public Safety
- [ ] The county sheriff's office alone
- [ ] The National Highway Traffic Safety Administration
- [ ] Each individual municipality's police department

**Explanation:** 550.063 requires collision report forms to be approved jointly by TxDOT (the "department" for this subchapter) and DPS.

---

### Q763 — Crash Investigation Procedures
**LO:** 22.30 · **Source:** BPOC 22.30

Among the standard traffic crash investigation equipment items listed for BPOC training — tape measure, rolo-tape or laser measuring device, chalk/crayon/paint, flares or warning markers, flashlight, sketch paper, and a traffic vest — the tape measure is typically specified at a length of ______ feet.

**Answer:** 100

**Explanation:** BPOC's equipment list specifies a 100-foot tape measure among the standard crash-investigation tools, alongside a rolo-tape/laser device for longer or repeated measurements.

---

### Q764 — Crash Investigation Procedures
**LO:** 22.31 · **Source:** TC 546.001, BPOC 22.31

Procedures for responding to a traffic crash scene are governed in part by Transportation Code Chapter ______, which addresses the permissible conduct of an authorized emergency vehicle en route to a call.

**Answer:** 546

**Explanation:** TC Ch.546 sets out what an authorized emergency vehicle's operator may do while responding — e.g., exceeding the speed limit or disregarding regulations on direction of movement — subject to the duty-of-care limits in 546.005.

---

### Q765 — Crash Investigation Procedures
**LO:** 22.31 · **Source:** TC 546.007(c)-(d)

Under TC 546.007, when a firefighter closes lanes of a road or highway to protect the safety of persons or property at an incident, the closure must be limited to:

- [x] The affected lane(s) plus one additional lane, unless emergency personnel safety requires closing more
- [ ] No more than half of the roadway's total lanes, regardless of the incident
